# AGENTS.md

给 AI 协作者 / 新贡献者的工程约定。**动手前先读本文**;本文与实际代码不符时,以代码为准并顺手修正本文。

## 项目速览

- 单页 3D 探索 Demo:`index.html` + 原生 ES Modules + Three.js **r160**(npm 依赖 `three@0.160.1`),Vite 7 构建
- 工程化工具链:Node >= 18、npm、Vite 7、ESLint 9(flat config)、Prettier 3、Vitest 3;配置见根目录 `vite.config.js` / `eslint.config.js` / `.prettierrc.json` / `vitest.config.js`
- 纯静态站点:没有后端;`npm run dev` 走 Vite HMR,`npm run build` 产出 `dist/`
- 全部 UI 文案为中文,代码注释也是中文;技术术语保留英文原文

## 运行与验证

```bash
npm install    # 首次安装
npm run dev    # 开发服务器,默认 http://localhost:5173(HMR)
npm run check  # lint + format:check + test
npm run build  # 生产构建,产物 dist/
```

不要用 `file://` 直接打开 `index.html`:裸模块 `three`、`import.meta.env` 与 `public/` 资产都依赖 Vite(dev)或打包产物(build)来解析。

验证方式(自动化只有 `tests/` 下的单测,画面与交互仍需手工过一遍):

1. `npm run dev`,确认加载遮罩消失、R-7 的欢迎对白 1.4s 后自动弹出
2. 控制台检查无 `[player] Soldier.glb load failed` 等错误;Google Fonts 拉取失败不算致命
3. 用调试句柄查场景状态:`__neon.scene` / `__neon.camera` / `__neon.player.debug()` / `__neon.island.obstacles.length`
4. 走动一圈验证:贴地、岩石/晶簇/塔/集装箱碰撞、能量边界、E 交互、设置面板、音效开关
5. 改了 `utils.js` / `keybinds.js` / `collision.js` 就补跑 `npm run test`;改了构建或资源路径再跑一次 `npm run build` + `npm run preview`

## 目录与模块职责

| 文件                     | 职责                                                                                    | 不该放什么                          |
| ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- |
| `scripts/main.js`        | 唯一的入口:渲染器/光照/相机/输入/主循环/对白数据                                        | 场景几何、UI 细节实现、碰撞数学     |
| `scripts/collision.js`   | 纯函数碰撞:入口 `moveWithCollision`(边界钳制 + "圆 vs 圆"推出),只依赖 utils             | 主循环状态、场景内容                |
| `scripts/island.js`      | 世界生成,返回 `{ interactives, obstacles, update }`                                     | 输入、相机、DOM                     |
| `scripts/player.js`      | 角色模型 + 骨骼动画 + 尘土,暴露 `update(dt, speed, t, turnRate, backpedal, air)`        | 移动计算(位移由 main.js 算)         |
| `scripts/postfx.js`      | 后期处理管线,暴露 `{ setSize, render, uniforms }`                                       | 场景内容                            |
| `scripts/audio.js`       | 程序化脚步音效(懒初始化 AudioContext)                                                   | 播放策略 / 用户手势判断(在 main.js) |
| `scripts/keybinds.js`    | 键位纯数据层:默认值、存储、冲突互换、预设                                               | 任何 DOM 操作                       |
| `scripts/assets.js`      | Canvas 程序化贴图工厂                                                                   | 场景布局                            |
| `scripts/utils.js`       | 常量、`heightAt`、`assetUrl`、数学工具                                                  | 有状态的东西                        |
| `scripts/ui/*.js`        | 各 HUD 组件,均返回 `{ ... }` 方法对象,**不 import three**                               | 游戏逻辑                            |
| `scripts/wasteland/*.js` | 第二条入口 `wasteland.html` 的独立玩法(格子/寻路/感知/负重/存档),见 `docs/wasteland.md` | 霓虹岛入口的任何逻辑                |
| `tests/*.test.js`        | Vitest 单测:高度场/数学工具、键位数据层、碰撞解算、废土竖切(格子/寻路/感知/负重/存档)   | 依赖 WebGL 的渲染与交互             |

依赖方向单向:`main.js` → 各模块 → `utils.js` / `assets.js` / `keybinds.js`;碰撞解算单独在 `collision.js`(只依赖 `utils.js` 的常量与 `heightAt`),`main.js` 只调它的 `moveWithCollision`。模块之间不要互相 import(除 `player.js` 引 `utils.js` 的常量)。

## 硬性约定(踩过坑的)

1. **高度场只有一个来源**:任何"贴地"的坐标都必须用 `heightAt(x, z)`(`scripts/utils.js`)。地形网格、角色 Y、相机防穿地、雷达海岸线都共用它,自己另写一份高度公式会造成"看着在地上、实际穿模"。
2. **实心道具必须登记碰撞**:`island.js` 里凡是"看起来挡人"的物体,都要 `addObstacle(x, z, r, top)`。`top` 是顶面高度,主循环用 `footY >= top` 跳过矮物(膝盖以下的碎石不挡路)。
3. **交互物只登记不实现对白**:`interactives.push({ name, kind, pos })` 里的 `kind`(`terminal` / `npc` / `core`)是 `main.js` 里 `dialogData` 的 key。新增交互类型要同时改这两处,否则按 E 没反应。
4. **色调映射在合成 pass 里**:渲染到 RenderTarget 时 `renderer.toneMapping` 不生效,所以 `renderer.toneMapping = NoToneMapping` + ACES 写在 `postfx.js` 的合成着色器。改画质时不要在这里加回 `ACESFilmicToneMapping`。
5. **随机数要确定性**:场景散布用 `rng(20240521)` 的 mulberry32(`scripts/utils.js`)。往里插新的 `rand()` 调用会改变后续所有物体的位置(整岛重排),属于可接受但需在提交里说明的改动。注释里保留"先取值保持随机序列一致"这类说明(`scripts/island.js` 的晶簇段落)。
6. **光照单位**:Three r155+ 用物理光照单位,`DirectionalLight` 强度是个位数(太阳 3.6),`PointLight` 是高数值(塔顶灯 70,decay=2)。不要拿旧教程里的 0.5 往上套。
7. **存储键集中声明**:`neon-isle:sound` / `neon-isle:look-mode` / `neon-isle:look-sens` / `neon-isle:keybinds`。读写一律 `try/catch` 包裹(隐私模式下 `localStorage` 会抛),失败时静默用默认值。
8. **音频只在用户手势里解锁**:首帧不创建 `AudioContext`,用 `{ once: true }` 的 `keydown`/`pointerdown`/`touchstart` 触发 `unlock()`。别在启动阶段直接 `new AudioContext()`。
9. **触屏判定**:启动看 `matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)')`,首次 `touchstart` 也会切到触屏模式(`body.touch-mode`)。触屏专用元素默认 `hidden`,由 CSS/JS 控制显隐,不要靠媒体查询硬切 DOM。

## 代码风格

- 2 空格缩进、单引号、必写分号;函数式模块(工厂函数返回方法对象),不用 class
- 格式化与静态检查以 `.prettierrc.json` / `eslint.config.js` 为准:改完跑 `npm run format` 与 `npm run lint`,不要手工对表格、缩进或引号风格
- 注释用中文,块首用 `// ============ 标题 ============`;解释"为什么"而不是"做了什么"
- 事件监听成对出现(`addEventListener` / 对应的清理或 `once`),指针手势用 `setPointerCapture` + `pointercancel` 兜底
- DOM 查询集中在 `main.js` 的 `els` 对象里,组件只接收元素引用,不自己 `querySelector` 全局
- 生产代码不留 `console.log`(ESLint `no-console` 只放行 `warn` / `error`);确需诊断用 `__neon` 调试句柄或临时 `console.warn` + `TODO`

## 提交

- 提交前自检:`npm run check` 全绿(lint + format:check + test);动了构建、资源路径或 `index.html` 再跑一遍 `npm run build`
- 提交信息 `<type>(<scope>): <subject>`,type 取 feat/fix/docs/style/refactor/perf/chore,scope 用模块名(`island` / `player` / `postfx` / `ui` / `collision`)
- 一次提交只做一件事;依赖升级(如 `three` / `vite` 大版本)单独成提交,写清变更要点与回滚方式

## 常见改动指引

| 想做的事         | 改哪里                                                                                                     |
| ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 加一个可交互物件 | `island.js` 的交互物段落(`interactives.push` 处)→ `dialogData`(`main.js`)→ 必要时 `addObstacle`            |
| 调整小岛形态     | `utils.js` 的 `heightAt`(会同时影响地形网格、碰撞、雷达)                                                   |
| 改碰撞规则       | `scripts/collision.js`(纯函数)+ `tests/` 下对应的单测                                                      |
| 加一种 HUD 元素  | `index.html` 加 DOM + `styles/hud.css` 加样式(用 `--c-*` token)+ `scripts/ui/` 新组件 + `main.js` 的 `els` |
| 换 / 加静态资产  | 文件放 `public/`,源码里用 `assetUrl('models/xxx.glb')` 引用(不要写死 `/models/xxx.glb`)                    |
| 调画质 / 氛围    | `postfx.js` 的 uniforms 表、`main.js` 的曝光与光照参数                                                     |
| 加音效           | `audio.js` 复用 `burst()`,注意 `ensure()` 仍要懒初始化                                                     |
| 加键位动作       | `keybinds.js` 的 `KEY_ACTIONS` + `main.js` 的 `keydown` 分支 + 需要时补 `data-bind` 键帽                   |
| 调跳跃手感       | `utils.js` 的 `GRAVITY` / `JUMP_SPEED`(主循环的垂直状态机在 `main.js` 的"垂直"段落)                        |

详细说明见 [`docs/`](docs/README.md)。

Let me write `_skin` carefully.

```python
def _skin(bm, xs, zs, mask, y0, thick, rng, jitter, shift=None, back_shift=None):
    """把 mask 描述的格子做成一块厚 thick 的闭合板：正面 y=y0，背面 y=y0+thick。

    shift[j] 是该行正面的额外 y 偏移（楼板带外凸），back_shift[j] 是背面额外偏移。
    """
    nx = len(xs) - 1
    nz = len(zs) - 1
    fv = {}
    bv = {}
    def fvert(i, j):
        k = (i, j)
        if k not in fv:
            dy = (shift[j] if shift else 0.0)
            fv[k] = bm.verts.new((xs[i] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter),
                                  y0 + dy + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) * 0.9,
                                  zs[j] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter)))
        return fv[k]
    def bvert(i, j):
        k = (i, j)
        if k not in bv:
            dy = (back_shift[j] if back_shift else 0.0)
            bv[k] = bm.verts.new((xs[i] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter),
                                  y0 + thick + dy + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) * 0.9,
                                  zs[j] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter)))
        return bv[k]
```
Problem: front and back verts at the same (i,j) get independent vertex jitter → the side walls won't be planar, that's fine. But jitter on x/z means front and back quad edges don't align → the side wall quads are non-planar (fine, they're quads; Blender triangulates). But there's a subtlety: adjacent cells share corner verts via the dict ✓ (same object per (i,j)) → no leaks. 

Hmm, but jitter per vertex keyed (i,j) means the *front* face of cell (i,j) uses fvert(i,j), fvert(i+1,j), fvert(i+1,j+1), fvert(i,j+1) ✓ shared with neighbors ✓ good.

Wait, but jitter on the *interior* vertex positions with shift per row: a vertex on the band boundary row belongs to both a band row and a non-band row → the shift[j] uses the vertex's own row index j → the boundary vertex gets one shift value, so the transition is a slope over one row. To make crisp, I insert paired rows: rows at 3.10-0.02 and 3.10+0.02, then shift applies to rows whose z center is inside the band → boundary vertex at z=3.10 is shared by both sides → it will take the shift of the row it's created from (i.e., shift[j] where j is the vertex's row index) → the vertex at exactly the boundary belongs to rows j and j+1; whichever is created first determines it. So the step would be smeared over the 0.04 m gap row — that's exactly what I want (68° slope).

Hmm, but if the shift only applies at vertex level with the row index, then a vertex at the band's lower boundary (z=3.08) gets shift[j=lower row]=0 and the vertex at 3.12 gets shift=0.09 → the face between them spans 0.04 m and is nearly vertical ✓ crisp.

Now the faces: 
- front quad for each solid cell: winding should give normal -Y (outward from the front). Vertices ordered (i,j),(i,j+1),(i+1,j+1),(i+1,j) gives normal... Let's compute: a=(xs[i],0,zs[j]), b=(xs[i],0,zs[j+1]), c=(xs[i+1],0,zs[j+1]) → (b-a)=(0,0,+dz), (c-b)=(+dx,0,0) → cross = (b-a)×(c-b) = (0,0,dz)×(dx,0,0) = (0*0-dz*0, dz*dx-0*0, 0*0-0*dx) = (0, dz*dx, 0) → +Y. But we want the front face normal to be -Y. However, I run recalc_face_normals + shell fix at the end, which handles orientation. So winding doesn't matter much as long as the shell is closed ✓. I'll rely on recalc + _fix_shell_normals (already proven).

- back quad similarly.
- side walls: for each cell and each of 4 directions where the neighbor is not solid: quad (f_a, f_b, b_b, b_a) using the two corner verts along the shared edge.

Let me write the boundary loops: for cell (i,j):
  - left neighbor (i-1): edge corners (i,j)-(i,j+1). If not solid(neighbor) → wall quad from f(i,j), f(i,j+1) to b(i,j), b(i,j+1).
  - right: corners (i+1,j)-(i+1,j+1), if cell (i+1,j) not solid.
  - bottom: corners (i,j)-(i+1,j), if (i,j-1) not solid.
  - top: corners (i,j+1)-(i+1,j+1), if (i,j+1) not solid.
Where solid(i,j) = in-range and mask[j][i].

This is a standard "extruded mask" and gives a closed shell ✓ (same as _solidify but with per-row shifts and correct shared verts).

Note: with this scheme, adjacent cells at the boundary of the mask get walls; the mesh is manifold unless pinch configurations (handled by _break_pinches) ✓.

Now, `_top_profile(nx, w, h, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right)`: with the segment logic from the previous version. I don't have that code in current file (I need to write it fresh). Requirements:
- jag (m) overall raggedness
- 2-4 wide segments with different heights
- clamp: any column at most 2 cells higher than both neighbors

Implementation (in *cell* units, then convert to z):
```python
def _top_profile(nx, dx, h, rng, jag, drop_left=0.0, drop_right=0.0, cell_z=None):
    nz = max(2, int(round(h / (cell_z or 0.26))))
    base = nz
    # 分段高差
    nseg = max(2, min(4, nx // 4))
    cut = sorted(rng.sample(range(3, max(4, nx - 3)), min(nseg - 1, max(0, nx - 7))))
    H = [0] * nx
    cur = base
    idx = 0
    for i in range(nx):
        while idx < len(cut) and i == cut[idx]:
            cur += rng.randint(-5, 5)  # ±1.6 m
            idx += 1
        H[i] = cur
    # 崩口：2-4 处，宽 2..nx//4 格，深 2..nz//5 格
    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 4)):
        wd = rng.randint(2, max(2, nx // 4))
        dep = rng.randint(2, max(2, nz // 5))
        x0 = rng.randint(0, max(0, nx - wd))
        for i in range(x0, min(nx, x0 + wd)):
            H[i] = max(2, H[i] - dep)
    # 小齿 25%
    for i in range(nx):
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            H[i] += rng.choice((-1, 1))
    # 两端下垂
    for i in range(nx):
        t = ...
    # 钳制：不离邻列超过 2 格
    for _ in range(6):
        changed = False
        for i in range(nx):
            lo = H[i-1] if i > 0 else H[i]
            hi = H[i+1] if i < nx-1 else H[i]
            lim = min(lo, hi) + 2
            if H[i] > lim:
                H[i] = lim; changed = True
        if not changed: break
    return [min(max(2, H[i]), nz) * dz for i in range(nx)]  # z 值
```
Wait, H in cell units relative to the base = h. dz = h/nz. So top z = H[i]*dz - ... careful: zs are the panel z coords 0..h where 0 = -BURY. Hmm, if I build zs from -BURY, z=0 in panel coords = -BURY; H counts *cells from the panel bottom* = -BURY. So top z (world) = -BURY + H*dz. Since h = top + BURY and dz = h/nz with nz cells, H=nz → top z = top ✓.

Then drop_left/drop_right in metres: subtract a falloff at the ends.

Let me simplify: H_total cells = nz, top cell count H[i] ∈ [2, nz].

Fine. Let me write it as: `_top_cells(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right)` returning ints, then z = -BURY + H*dz. And jag influences the崩口 depth.

Let me reconstruct `_to_object` (from memory): 
```python
def _to_object(name, bm, mats, coll, loc=LOC, rot_z=ROT_Z):
    bm.normal_update()
    bmesh.ops.recalc_face_normals(bm, faces=bm.faces[:])
    _fix_shell_normals(bm)
    me = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
    bm.to_mesh(me)
    bm.free()
    for m in mats: me.materials.append(m)
    ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name, me)
    ob.location = loc
    ob.rotation_euler = (0.0, 0.0, rot_z)
    coll.objects.link(ob)
    return ob
```
`_fix_shell_normals(bm)` = flood fill components, compute signed volume, reverse if negative.

`_purge()`: remove objects with PREFIX, orphan meshes, unused wst_mat_* materials.

`build()`: ensure collection, purge, build each part, create objects, report. It printed "HEADLESS " + json.dumps(report) single line.

`_report(created)`: computes created list, tris, bbox (world), materials.

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 轴 / 掩码

def _axis(w, cell):
    n = max(2, int(round(w / cell)))
    dx = w / n
    return [i * dx for i in range(n + 1)], dx


def _zaxis(h, cell, band=None):
    """z 轴：均匀网格；band=(z0,z1) 时在边界插一对很近的行，形成硬朗的楼板带台阶。"""
    n = max(2, int(round(h / cell)))
    dz = h / n
    zs = [i * dz for i in range(n + 1)]
    if band:
        extra = []
        for zb in band:
            if 0.0 < zb < h:
                extra += [zb - 0.02, zb + 0.02]
        if extra:
            zs = sorted(set([round(v, 5) for v in zs] + [round(v, 5) for v in extra]))
    return zs
```
Careful: zs must start at 0 (panel bottom) — but the panel bottom is at world -BURY. I'll build zs from 0..h and translate the whole wall by -BURY inside `_wall` (matrix translation). And pass band coords already shifted: band_panel = (BAND_Z0 + BURY, BAND_Z1 + BURY).

Simpler: build zs from 0..h where panel z=0 → world -BURY; then `_wall` applies `mat @ Translation(0,0,-BURY)`. Band in panel coords = BAND_Z0 + BURY.

Now `_mask`:
```python
def _mask(xs, zs, tops, holes, rng, erode_side=0.0):
    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1
    mask = [[False] * nx for _ in range(nz)]
    for j in range(nz):
        cz = 0.5 * (zs[j] + zs[j + 1])
        for i in range(nx):
            cx = 0.5 * (xs[i] + xs[i + 1])
            ok = cz <= tops[i]
            if ok and erode_side > 0.0 and (i == 0 or i == nx - 1):
                if rng.random() < erode_side:
                    ok = False
            if ok:
                for (ax, bx, az, bz) in holes:
                    if ax < cx < bx and az < cz < bz:
                        ok = False
                        break
            mask[j][i] = ok
    return mask
```
`tops` must be per column, in panel z. But `tops` is computed for the front grid; the rear grid has different nx → I sample tops at its column centers.

Hole x coords are panel x ✓; z coords panel z (= world z + BURY). I'll pass holes in world z and convert: hole z panel = z + BURY. Let me just pass holes already in panel coords from `_wall`.

```python
def _wall(bm, mat, w, top, holes, rng, cell=0.26, through=(), jag=1.1,
          drop_left=0.0, drop_right=0.0, erode_side=0.0, jitter=0.045,
          band=(BAND_Z0, BAND_Z1), rear=True):
    h = top + BURY
    xs, dx = _axis(w, cell)
    nx = len(xs) - 1
    nz = max(2, int(round(h / cell)))
    zs = _zaxis(h, cell, (band[0] + BURY, band[1] + BURY) if band else None)
    tops = _top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h)
    holes_p = [(a, b, c + BURY, d + BURY) for (a, b, c, d) in holes]
    through_p = [holes_p[i] for i in through]
    m_f = _mask(xs, zs, tops, holes_p, rng, erode_side)
    _break_pinches(m_f, nx, len(zs) - 1)
    _keep_largest_island(m_f, nx, len(zs) - 1)
    zs_f = zs
    # 楼板带外凸：正面 -0.09
    shift_f = _band_shift(zs_f, band, BURY, -0.09)
    _skin(bm, xs, zs_f, m_f, 0.0, FRONT_SKIN, rng, jitter, shift=shift_f)
    if rear:
        xs2, _ = _axis(w, 0.55)
        zs2 = _zaxis(h, 0.55, ...)  # band?
        tops2 = [ _sample(tops, xs, xs2[i]+(xs2[i+1]-xs2[i])*0.5) - 0.10 for i ...]
        m_r = _mask(xs2, zs2, tops2, through_p, rng, 0.0)
        _break_pinches... _keep_largest_island...
        shift_b = _band_shift(zs2, band, BURY, +0.09)
        _skin(bm, xs2, zs2, m_r, REAR_Y0, REAR_T, rng, jitter*1.6, back_shift=shift_b)
    ...
```

`_band_shift(zs, band, bury, dy)`:
```python
def _band_shift(zs, band, bury, dy):
    if not band:
        return None
    b0, b1 = band[0] + bury, band[1] + bury
    return [dy if (b0 <= 0.5*(zs[j]+zs[j+1]) <= b1) else 0.0 for j in range(len(zs))]
```
Note: the shift is per *row index j* in the vertex dict, and verts are keyed (i,j) with j = row index of the vertex (0..nz). So vertex j gets shift[j] (using row center for j<nz; for j=nz use row nz-1). I'll compute `shift` array of length nz+1: for vertex j, use the row j (if j<nz) else j-1. Simpler: pass a per-vertex shift list of length nz+1 built from vertex z: `dy if b0 <= zs[j] <= b1 else 0`. Since I inserted paired rows at b0±0.02, verts exactly at b0 get... Let me use vertex z directly: shift = dy if (b0 + 0.021 <= zs[j] <= b1 - 0.021)?? 

Hmm. Let me think about which rows should protrude: rows whose *cell* is inside the band. With paired rows at b0-0.02, b0+0.02 and b1-0.02, b1+0.02:
cells: [.., b0-0.02] (normal), [b0-0.02, b0+0.02] (transition), [b0+0.02, b1-0.02] (band), [b1-0.02, b1+0.02] (transition), [b1+0.02, ..] (normal).
Vertex shift should be: at z <= b0+0.02 → 0; at z >= b0+0.02 and <= b1-0.02 → dy; at z >= b1-0.02 → 0.
So `shift[j] = dy if b0+0.02 < zs[j] < b1-0.02 else 0` — the transition cell [b0-0.02, b0+0.02] then has front verts at 0 and dy → a steep quad ✓ (0.04 m run, 0.09 rise = 66°) ✓ crisp.

Good: `shift[j] = dy if (b0 + 0.019 < zs[j] < b1 - 0.019) else 0.0`.

Next `_top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h)`:
```python
def _top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h):
    dz = h / nz
    H = [nz] * nx
    # 2-4 段宽平台
    nseg = max(2, min(4, nx // 4))
    if nx >= 8 and nseg > 1:
        cuts = sorted(rng.sample(range(3, nx - 2), nseg - 1))
        idx, cur = 0, nz
        for i in range(nx):
            while idx < len(cuts) and i == cuts[idx]:
                cur = min(nz, max(3, cur + rng.randint(-5, 5)))
                idx += 1
            H[i] = cur
    # 2-4 处大崩口
    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 4)):
        wd = rng.randint(2, max(2, nx // 4))
        dep = rng.randint(2, max(2, int(round(jag / dz)) + 2))
        x0 = rng.randint(0, max(0, nx - wd))
        for i in range(x0, min(nx, x0 + wd)):
            H[i] = max(3, H[i] - dep)
    # 25% 列小齿 ±1 格
    for i in range(nx):
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            H[i] = min(nz, max(3, H[i] + rng.choice((-1, 1))))
    # 两端下垂
    for i in range(nx):
        fl = max(0.0, 1.0 - i / max(1.0, nx * 0.22))
        fr = max(0.0, 1.0 - (nx - 1 - i) / max(1.0, nx * 0.22))
        H[i] -= int(round((drop_left * fl + drop_right * fr) / dz))
        H[i] = max(3, min(nz, H[i]))
    # 细高片钳制：最多比左右邻列高 2 格
    for _ in range(8):
        ch = False
        for i in range(nx):
            lo = H[i - 1] if i > 0 else H[i]
            hi = H[i + 1] if i < nx - 1 else H[i]
            lim = min(lo, hi) + 2
            if H[i] > lim:
                H[i] = lim
                ch = True
        if not ch:
            break
    return [H[i] * dz for i in range(nx)]
```
Note rng.sample needs nseg-1 <= nx-5; guard with `if nx >= 8`.

- L back stub: mat = Translation((LEFT_X0, BACK_Y - WALL_T, 0)); w = 4.90; top 5.40.

Similarly the L end wall: mat = Translation((LEFT_X0, BACK_Y, 0)) @ Rotation(-90°, Z) → local x = BACK_Y - y, local y (panel +Y) maps to... Rotation(-90°,Z) maps local (x,y,0) → ? Rotation(-90°) : x' = x cos(-90) - y sin(-90) = y; y' = x sin(-90) + y cos(-90) = -x. So local (x,y) → world (y + tx, -x + ty) = (y + LEFT_X0, BACK_Y - x). ```python
def _tri_prism(bm, p0, p1, p2, nrm, t=0.012):
    a = [bm.verts.new(p + nrm * (t * 0.5)) for p in (p0, p1, p2)]
    b = [bm.verts.new(p - nrm * (t * 0.5)) for p in (p0, p1, p2)]
    bm.faces.new(a); bm.faces.new(list(reversed(b)))
    for i in range(3):
        j = (i + 1) % 3
        bm.faces.new((a[i], b[i], b[j], a[j]))
```

p_i = p0 + d*L*(t_i) + b*L*(t_i^2)*0.5 + jitter. Then a hook: last 2 points turn.

I'll implement:
```python
def _rebar_pts(start, d, b, length, rng, hook=0.18):
    d = Vector(d).normalized()
    b = Vector(b)
    n = 7
    pts = [start]
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        t = i / n
        p = start + d * (length * t) + b * (length * (t ** 1.8))
        p += Vector((rng.uniform(-0.02, 0.02), rng.uniform(-0.02, 0.02), rng.uniform(-0.02, 0.02)))
        pts.append(p)
    # 末端弯钩
    tip = pts[-1]
    last_dir = (pts[-1] - pts[-2]).normalized()
    side = last_dir.cross(Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))
    if side.length < 1e-4:
        side = Vector((1.0, 0.0, 0.0))
    side.normalize()
    pts.append(tip + side * hook)
    pts.append(tip + side * hook - last_dir * (hook * 0.55))
    return pts
```
Root: `root = start - d*REBAR_BURY` and prepend, then `_tube(bm, [root] + pts, 0.0075, sides=5)`.

sides=5 → 5 verts per ring; caps need fans. `_tube` already does caps (saw its beginning; it builds rings and caps). I'll write my own `_tube` to be safe:

```python
def _tube(bm, pts, radius, sides=6):
    rings = []
    up = Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0))
    for i, p in enumerate(pts):
        if i == 0:
            d = (pts[1] - pts[0])
        elif i == len(pts) - 1:
            d = (pts[-1] - pts[-2])
        else:
            d = (pts[i + 1] - pts[i - 1])
        d.normalize()
        ref = up if abs(d.dot(up)) < 0.9 else Vector((1.0, 0.0, 0.0))
        u = d.cross(ref).normalized()
        v = d.cross(u).normalized()
        ring = []
        for k in range(sides):
            a = 2.0 * math.pi * k / sides
            ring.append(bm.verts.new(p + u * (radius * math.cos(a)) + v * (radius * math.sin(a))))
        rings.append(ring)
    for i in range(len(rings) - 1):
        a, b = rings[i], rings[i + 1]
        for k in range(sides):
            k2 = (k + 1) % sides
            bm.faces.new((a[k], a[k2], b[k2], b[k]))
    bm.faces.new(list(reversed(rings[0])))
    bm.faces.new(rings[-1])
    return
```
Note: per-ring frames using "up" reference can twist between rings but for thin tubes it's fine (no visible seam issue since faces connect a[k]→b[k] regardless). ✓ Plus recalc normals handles orientation.

Now tri count per rebar: 9 rings × 5 sides × 2 = 90 + caps (5-gon → 3 tris each = 6) ≈ 96 ✓ ×10 = 960.

---------------------------------------------------------------- 网格骨架\n\ndef _merge(dst, src, mat):\n    \"\"\"把 src bmesh 中的几何按 mat 变换后并入 dst。\"\"\"\n    vmap = {}\n    for v in src.verts:\n        vmap[v] = dst.verts.new(mat @ v.co)\n    for f in src.faces:\n        try:\n            dst.faces.new([vmap[v] for v in f.verts])\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    src.free()\n\n\ndef _axis(w, cell):\n    n = max(2, int(round(w / cell)))\n    dx = w / n\n    return [i * dx for i in range(n + 1)], dx\n\n\ndef _zaxis(h, cell, band=None):\n    \"\"\"z 轴：均匀网格；(band) 边界插一对相隔 4 cm 的行 → 楼板带台阶是硬棱不是斜面。\"\"\"\n    n = max(2, int(round(h / cell)))\n    dz = h / n\n    zs = [i * dz for i in range(n + 1)]\n    if band:\n        extra = []\n        for zb in band:\n            if 0.0 < zb < h:\n                extra += [zb - 0.02, zb + 0.02]\n        if extra:\n            zs = sorted(set([round(v, 5) for v in zs] + [round(v, 5) for v in extra]))\n    return zs\n\n\ndef _top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h):\n    \"\"\"墙顶轮廓（每列保留几格）：2–4 段宽平台 + 2–4 处大崩口 + 少量 1 格小齿。\n\n    只允许\"1 列宽的细高片\"存在到 ±2 格：细高片在成片里就是一张发亮薄卡片。\n    \"\"\"\n    dz = h / nz\n    H = [nz] * nx\n    nseg = max(2, min(4, nx // 4))\n    if nx >= 8 and nseg > 1:\n        cuts = sorted(rng.sample(range(3, nx - 2), nseg - 1))\n        idx, cur = 0, nz\n        for i in range(nx):\n            while idx < len(cuts) and i == cuts[idx]:\n                cur = min(nz, max(3, cur + rng.randint(-5, 5)))\n                idx += 1\n            H[i] = cur\n\n    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 4)):\n        wd = rng.randint(2, max(2, nx // 4))\n        dep = rng.randint(2, max(2, int(round(jag / dz)) + 2))\n        x0 = rng.randint(0, max(0, nx - wd))\n        for i in range(x0, min(nx, x0 + wd)):\n            H[i] = max(3, H[i] - dep)\n\n    # 一处深崩口（打到楼板带，露出楼板断面）\n    wd = rng.randint(2, max(2, nx // 5))\n    x0 = rng.randint(nx // 2, max(nx // 2, nx - wd))\n    for i in range(x0, min(nx, x0 + wd)):\n        H[i] = max(3, H[i] - int(round(nz * 0.55)))\n\n    for i in range(nx):\n        if rng.random() < 0.25:\n            H[i] = min(nz, max(3, H[i] + rng.choice((-1, 1))))\n\n    span = max(1.0, nx * 0.22)\n    for i in range(nx):\n        fl = max(0.0, 1.0 - i / span)\n        fr = max(0.0, 1.0 - (nx - 1 - i) / span)\n        H[i] -= int(round((drop_left * fl + drop_right * fr) / dz))\n        H[i] = max(3, min(nz, H[i]))\n\n    for _ in range(8):\n        changed = False\n        for i in range(nx):\n            lo = H[i - 1] if i > 0 else H[i]\n            hi = H[i + 1] if i < nx - 1 else H[i]\n            lim = min(lo, hi) + 2\n            if H[i] > lim:\n                H[i] = lim\n                changed = True\n        if not changed:\n            break\n    return [H[i] * dz for i in range(nx)]\n\n\ndef _mask(xs, zs, tops, holes, rng, erode_side=0.0):\n    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1\n    mask = [[False] * nx for _ in range(nz)]\n    for j in range(nz):\n        cz = 0.5 * (zs[j] + zs[j + 1])\n        for i in range(nx):\n            cx = 0.5 * (xs[i] + xs[i + 1])\n            ok = cz <= tops[i]\n            if ok and erode_side > 0.0 and (i == 0 or i == nx - 1):\n                if rng.random() < erode_side:\n                    ok = False\n            if ok:\n                for (ax, bx, az, bz) in holes:\n                    if ax < cx < bx and az < cz < bz:\n                        ok = False\n                        break\n            mask[j][i] = ok\n    return mask\n\n\ndef _break_pinches(mask, nx, nz):\n    \"\"\"拆掉\"只在一个角点上相接\"的格子对（否则加厚后出现 4 面共边的非流形棱）。\"\"\"\n    for _ in range(8):\n        changed = False\n        for j in range(1, nz):\n            for i in range(1, nx):\n                a, b = mask[j - 1][i - 1], mask[j - 1][i]\n                c, d = mask[j][i - 1], mask[j][i]\n                if a and d and not b and not c:\n                    mask[j][i] = False\n                    changed = True\n                elif b and c and not a and not d:\n                    mask[j - 1][i] = False\n                    changed = True\n        if not changed:\n            return\n\n\ndef _keep_largest_island(mask, nx, nz):\n    \"\"\"只留最大的一块连通区域（拆夹点可能孤立出 1–2 格，加厚后就是浮碎片）。\"\"\"\n    seen = [[False] * nx for _ in range(nz)]\n    keep = set()\n    for j0 in range(nz):\n        for i0 in range(nx):\n            if not mask[j0][i0] or seen[j0][i0]:\n                continue\n            seen[j0][i0] = True\n            stack = [(j0, i0)]\n            comp = []\n            while stack:\n                j, i = stack.pop()\n                comp.append((j, i))\n                for dj, di in ((1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, 1), (0, -1)):\n                    jj, ii = j + dj, i + di\n                    if 0 <= jj < nz and 0 <= ii < nx and mask[jj][ii] and not seen[jj][ii]:\n                        seen[jj][ii] = True\n                        stack.append((jj, ii))\n            if len(comp) > len(keep):\n                keep = set(comp)\n    for j in range(nz):\n        for i in range(nx):\n            if mask[j][i] and (j, i) not in keep:\n                mask[j][i] = False\n\n\ndef _band_shift(zs, band, sign):\n    \"\"\"楼板带外凸量（按顶点 z）：只在带内、离边界 2 cm 以上的行外凸。\"\"\"\n    if not band:\n        return None\n    b0, b1 = band\n    return [sign * BAND_OUT if (b0 + 0.019 < z < b1 - 0.019) else 0.0 for z in zs]\n\n\ndef _skin(bm, xs, zs, mask, y0, thick, rng, jitter, shift=None, back_shift=None):\n    \"\"\"把 mask 的格子做成一块厚 thick 的闭合板：正面 y=y0(+shift)，背面 y=y0+thick(+back_shift)。\"\"\"\n    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1\n    fv, bv = {}, {}\n\n    def fvert(i, j):\n        k = (i, j)\n        v = fv.get(k)\n        if v is None:\n            dy = shift[j] if shift else 0.0\n            v = bm.verts.new((xs[i] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter),\n                              y0 + dy + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) * 0.9,\n                              zs[j] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter)))\n            fv[k] = v\n        return v\n\n    def bvert(i, j):\n        k = (i, j)\n        v = bv.get(k)\n        if v is None:\n            dy = back_shift[j] if back_shift else 0.0\n            v = bm.verts.new((xs[i] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter),\n                              y0 + thick + dy + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) * 0.9,\n                              zs[j] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter)))\n            bv[k] = v\n        return v\n\n    def solid(i, j):\n        return 0 <= i < nx and 0 <= j < nz and mask[j][i]\n\n    for j in range(nz):\n        for i in range(nx):\n            if not mask[j][i]:\n                continue\n            try:\n                bm.faces.new((fvert(i, j), fvert(i, j + 1), fvert(i + 1, j + 1), fvert(i + 1, j)))\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n            try:\n                bm.faces.new((bvert(i + 1, j), bvert(i + 1, j + 1), bvert(i, j + 1), bvert(i, j)))\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n            # 侧壁：只在与空格相邻的一侧补，保证闭合且法线可 recalc\n            if not solid(i - 1, j):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i, j), bvert(i, j), bvert(i, j + 1), fvert(i, j + 1)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            if not solid(i + 1, j):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i + 1, j + 1), bvert(i + 1, j + 1), bvert(i + 1, j), fvert(i + 1, j)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            if not solid(i, j - 1):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i + 1, j), bvert(i + 1, j), bvert(i, j), fvert(i, j)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            if not solid(i, j + 1):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i, j + 1), bvert(i, j + 1), bvert(i + 1, j + 1), fvert(i + 1, j + 1)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n    loose = [v for v in bm.verts if not v.link_faces]\n    if loose:\n        bmesh.ops.delete(bm, geom=loose, context=\"VERTS\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 小构件\n\ndef _box(bm, center, size, rot=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), rng=None, jitter=0.0, mat_idx=0):\n    \"\"\"一个随机旋转/顶点抖动的盒子（12 三角）。\"\"\"\n    sx, sy, sz = size\n    mat = Matrix.Translation(center) @ Euler(rot, \"XYZ\").to_matrix().to_4x4()\n    corners = []\n    for dz in (-0.5, 0.5):\n        for dy in (-0.5, 0.5):\n            for dx in (-0.5, 0.5):\n                jx = rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) if rng else 0.0\n                jy = rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) if rng else 0.0\n                jz = rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) if rng else 0.0\n                corners.append(bm.verts.new(mat @ Vector((dx * sx + jx, dy * sy + jy, dz * sz + jz))))\n    quads = ((0, 1, 3, 2), (4, 6, 7, 5), (0, 4, 5, 1), (2, 3, 7, 6), (0, 2, 6, 4), (1, 5, 7, 3))\n    for q in quads:\n        f = bm.faces.new([corners[i] for i in q])\n        f.material_index = mat_idx\n\n\ndef _tri_prism(bm, p0, p1, p2, nrm, t=0.012):\n    \"\"\"薄三角片（碎玻璃）：两点面 + 三条侧边，闭合 8 三角。\"\"\"\n    a = [bm.verts.new(p + nrm * (t * 0.5)) for p in (p0, p1, p2)]\n    b = [bm.verts.new(p - nrm * (t * 0.5)) for p in (p0, p1, p2)]\n    bm.faces.new(a)\n    bm.faces.new(list(reversed(b)))\n    for i in range(3):\n        j = (i + 1) % 3\n        bm.faces.new((a[i], b[i], b[j], a[j]))\n\n\ndef _tube(bm, pts, radius, sides=5):\n    \"\"\"沿折线扫掠的圆管（两端封盖）。\"\"\"\n    up = Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0))\n    rings = []\n    for i, p in enumerate(pts):\n        if i == 0:\n            d = pts[1] - pts[0]\n        elif i == len(pts) - 1:\n            d = pts[-1] - pts[-2]\n        else:\n            d = pts[i + 1] - pts[i - 1]\n        if d.length < 1e-6:\n            d = Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0))\n        d = d.normalized()\n        ref = up if abs(d.dot(up)) < 0.9 else Vector((1.0, 0.0, 0.0))\n        u = d.cross(ref).normalized()\n        v = d.cross(u).normalized()\n        ring = []\n        for k in range(sides):\n            a = 2.0 * math.pi * k / sides\n            ring.append(bm.verts.new(p + u * (radius * math.cos(a)) + v * (radius * math.sin(a))))\n        rings.append(ring)\n    for i in range(len(rings) - 1):\n        a, b = rings[i], rings[i + 1]\n        for k in range(sides):\n            k2 = (k + 1) % sides\n            bm.faces.new((a[k], a[k2], b[k2], b[k]))\n    bm.faces.new(list(reversed(rings[0])))\n    bm.faces.new(rings[-1])\n\n\ndef _rot_low(size, rot):\n    m = Euler(rot, \"XYZ\").to_matrix()\n    sx, sy, sz = size\n    return 0.5 * (abs(m[2][0]) * sx + abs(m[2][1]) * sy + abs(m[2][2]) * sz)\n\n\ndef _lean(bm, kx, ky):\n    \"\"\"按高度剪切：造成 1–4° 倾角。\"\"\"\n    for v in bm.verts:\n        z = v.co.z\n        if z > 0.0:\n            v.co.x += kx * z\n            v.co.y += ky * z\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 出对象\n\ndef _ensure_collection():\n    root = bpy.data.collections.get(ROOT_COLLECTION)\n    if root is None:\n        root = bpy.data.collections.new(ROOT_COLLECTION)\n        bpy.context.scene.collection.children.link(root)\n    coll = bpy.data.collections.get(COLLECTION)\n    if coll is None:\n        coll = bpy.data.collections.new(COLLECTION)\n        root.children.link(coll)\n    return coll\n\n\ndef _purge():\n    for ob in list(bpy.data.objects):\n        if ob.name.startswith(PREFIX):\n            me = ob.data\n            bpy.data.objects.remove(ob, do_unlink=True)\n            if me is not None and me.users == 0:\n                bpy.data.meshes.remove(me)\n    for me in list(bpy.data.meshes):\n        if me.users == 0:\n            bpy.data.meshes.remove(me)\n    for mat in list(bpy.data.materials):\n        if mat.name.startswith(\"wst_mat_\") and mat.users == 0:\n            bpy.data.materials.remove(mat)\n\n\ndef _shell_signed_volume(faces):\n    vol = 0.0\n    for f in faces:\n        vs = f.verts\n        a = vs[0].co\n        for k in range(1, len(vs) - 1):\n            b, c = vs[k].co, vs[k + 1].co\n            vol += a.dot(b.cross(c)) / 6.0\n    return vol\n\n\ndef _fix_shell_normals(bm):\n    \"\"\"按连通分量校正法线：体积为负说明整片壳朝内（recalc 对薄壳会判反）。\"\"\"\n    bm.faces.ensure_lookup_table()\n    seen = set()\n    for f0 in bm.faces:\n        if f0 in seen:\n            continue\n        comp, stack = [], [f0]\n        seen.add(f0)\n        while stack:\n            f = stack.pop()\n            comp.append(f)\n            for e in f.edges:\n                for g in e.link_faces:\n                    if g not in seen:\n                        seen.add(g)\n                        stack.append(g)\n        if _shell_signed_volume(comp) < 0.0:\n            bmesh.ops.reverse_faces(bm, faces=comp)\n\n\ndef _to_object(name, bm, mats, coll):\n    bm.normal_update()\n    bmesh.ops.recalc_face_normals(bm, faces=bm.faces[:])\n    _fix_shell_normals(bm)\n    me = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)\n    bm.to_mesh(me)\n    bm.free()\n    for m in mats:\n        me.materials.append(m)\n    ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name, me)\n    ob.location = LOC\n    ob.rotation_euler = (0.0, 0.0, ROT_Z)\n    coll.objects.link(ob)\n    return ob\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 墙（两层皮 + 楼板带）\n\ndef _hole_bounds(mask, xs, zs, hole):\n    \"\"\"取某窗口实际吃掉的格子范围 → 洞壁的真实平面坐标。\"\"\"\n    ax, bx, az, bz = hole\n    i0 = i1 = None\n    j0 = j1 = None\n    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1\n    for j in range(nz):\n        cz = 0.5 * (zs[j] + zs[j + 1])\n        for i in range(nx):\n            cx = 0.5 * (xs[i] + xs[i + 1])\n            if ax < cx < bx and az < cz < bz and not mask[j][i]:\n                i0 = i if i0 is None else min(i0, i)\n                i1 = i if i1 is None else max(i1, i)\n                j0 = j if j0 is None else min(j0, j)\n                j1 = j if j1 is None else max(j1, j)\n    if i0 is None:\n        return None\n    return (xs[i0], xs[i1 + 1], zs[j0], zs[j1 + 1])\n\n\ndef _window_dress(bm, rng, bounds):\n    \"\"\"残存窗框细条 + 歪斜碎玻璃（都长在 0.14 深的窗洞里，贴着洞壁）。\"\"\"\n    x0, x1, z0, z1 = bounds\n    w = x1 - x0\n    h = z1 - z0\n    if w < 0.55 or h < 0.55:\n        return\n    ymid = FRONT_SKIN * 0.45\n    # 下槛（横向变形条）\n    _box(bm, Vector((0.5 * (x0 + x1), ymid, z0 + 0.045)),\n         (w, FRONT_SKIN * 0.7, 0.09), rot=(0.0, rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03), 0.0))\n    # 一根竖梃 / 一根上框残条\n    jx = x0 + w * rng.uniform(0.25, 0.7)\n    _box(bm, Vector((jx, ymid, z0 + h * 0.42)),\n         (0.055, FRONT_SKIN * 0.55, h * 0.62), rot=(rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05), 0.0, 0.0))\n    _box(bm, Vector((x0 + w * 0.5, ymid, z1 - 0.05)),\n         (w * 0.55, FRONT_SKIN * 0.5, 0.055), rot=(0.0, rng.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), 0.0))\n    # 碎玻璃：底边贴在下槛顶面（z0+0.09），三片歪斜三角形\n    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 3)):\n        bx = x0 + w * rng.uniform(0.12, 0.78)\n        by = 0.02 + rng.uniform(0.0, 0.06)\n        bw = w * rng.uniform(0.12, 0.3)\n        bh = h * rng.uniform(0.12, 0.34)\n        p0 = Vector((bx, by, z0 + 0.09))\n        p1 = Vector((bx + bw, by + rng.uniform(-0.02, 0.02), z0 + 0.09))\n        p2 = Vector((bx + bw * rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8), by + rng.uniform(-0.01, 0.03),\n                     z0 + 0.09 + bh))\n        _tri_prism(bm, p0, p1, p2, Vector((0.0, 1.0, 0.0)), t=0.010)\n\n\ndef _wall(bm, mat, w, top, holes, rng, cell=CELL_FRONT, through=(),\n          jag=1.1, drop_left=0.0, drop_right=0.0, erode_side=0.0,\n          jitter=0.045, band=(BAND_Z0, BAND_Z1), dress=(), rear=True):\n    \"\"\"一面墙：前皮（开窗口袋）+ 后皮（只有穿透洞）+ 楼板带外凸。\n\n    z 从 -BURY 到 top；y 从 0（外表面）到 WALL_T。hole 坐标是建筑局部 (x, z)。\n    \"\"\"\n    h = top + BURY\n    xs, dx = _axis(w, cell)\n    nx = len(xs) - 1\n    nz = max(2, int(round(h / cell)))\n    zs = _zaxis(h, cell, (band[0] + BURY, band[1] + BURY) if band else None)\n    nz = len(zs) - 1\n    tops = _top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h)\n    holes_p = [(a, b, c + BURY, d + BURY) for (a, b, c, d) in holes]\n    through_p = [holes_p[i] for i in through if 0 <= i < len(holes_p)]\n\n    m_f = _mask(xs, zs, tops, holes_p, rng, erode_side)\n    _break_pinches(m_f, nx, nz)\n    _keep_largest_island(m_f, nx, nz)\n    _skin(bm, xs, zs, m_f, 0.0, FRONT_SKIN, rng, jitter,\n          shift=_band_shift(zs, tuple(v + BURY for v in band) if band else None, -1.0))\n\n    if rear:\n        xs2, dx2 = _axis(w, CELL_REAR)\n        nx2 = len(xs2) - 1\n        zs2 = _zaxis(h, CELL_REAR, tuple(v + BURY for v in band) if band else None)\n        nz2 = len(zs2) - 1\n        tops2 = []\n        for i in range(nx2):\n            cx = 0.5 * (xs2[i] + xs2[i + 1])\n            k = min(nx - 1, max(0, int(cx / dx)))\n            tops2.append(tops[k] - 0.10)\n        m_r = _mask(xs2, zs2, tops2, through_p, rng, 0.0)\n        _break_pinches(m_r, nx2, nz2)\n        _keep_largest_island(m_r, nx2, nz2)\n        _skin(bm, xs2, zs2, m_r, REAR_Y0, REAR_T, rng, jitter * 1.5,\n              back_shift=_band_shift(zs2, tuple(v + BURY for v in band) if band else None, 1.0))\n\n    # 窗户配件（窗框残条 / 碎玻璃）\n    for hi in dress:\n        if 0 <= hi < len(holes_p):\n            b = _hole_bounds(m_f, xs, zs, holes_p[hi])\n            if b:\n                _window_dress(bm, rng, b)\n\n    _merge_into(bm, mat)\n\n\ndef _merge_into(bm, mat):\n    \"\"\"把 bm 里当前全部几何按 mat 变换（就地，用于把面板搬到墙位）。\"\"\"\n    if mat is None:\n        return\n    for v in bm.verts:\n        v.co = mat @ v.co\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 各部件\n\ndef _left_block():\n    \"\"\"左段：正面开窗两层残墙 + 可见左端墙 + 后墙残段。\"\"\"\n    bm = bmesh.new()\n    rng = random.Random(SEED + 1)\n    w = LEFT_X1 - LEFT_X0\n\n    holes = [\n        (0.38, 1.23, 1.00, 2.70),\n        (1.78, 2.63, 0.95, 2.65),\n        (3.18, 4.03, 1.10, 2.75),\n        (0.50, 1.45, 4.05, 5.65),\n        (1.95, 2.85, 4.00, 5.60),\n        (3.25, 4.65, 4.15, 5.70),\n    ]\n    sub =      [(4.30, 5.50, 1.20, 2.60),\n           (1.60, 2.80, 3.95, 5.35),\n           (6.55, 7.45, 4.10, 5.55)],\n          rng, cell=CELL_SIDE, through=(2,), jag=0.95,\n          drop_left=0.55, -BURY)))\n\n    # 后墙残段（大半塌掉，只作背光剪影）\n    sub = bmesh.new()\n    _wall(sub, None, w, 5.40,\n          [(0.55, 1.95, 0.60, 2.60), (2.60, 3.95, 1.30, 3.10)],\n          rng, cell=CELL_SIDE, jag=2.30, Matrix.Translation((0.0, 0.0, -BURY)))\n\n    _lean(bm, -0.022, -0.012)\n    return bm\n\n\ndef _mound_h(x, y):\n    \"\"\"碎砖坡高度场：中段最高 ~3.05 m，向四周衰减到 0。\"\"\"\n    xc, yc = 0.70, 0.30\n    ax, ay = 3.45, 6.60\n    t = 1.0 - ((x - xc) / ax) ** 2 - ((y - yc) / ay) ** 2\n    if t <= 0.0:\n        return 0.0\n    return 3.05 * (t ** 0.85)\n\n\nRUBBLE_CLASSES = ((0.15, 0.11), (0.40, 0.40), (0.90, 0.19))\n\n\ndef _rubble():\n    \"\"\"中段坍塌：坡状核心 + 三档碎块（大块半埋带倾角）+ 大残板 + 外围散砖。\"\"\"\n    bm = bmesh.new()\n    rng = random.Random(SEED + 3)\n\n    nx, ny = 18, 28\n    x0, x1 = -3.05, 4.45\n    y0, y1 = -5.40, 6.00\n    verts = {}\n    for j in range(ny + 1):\n        y = y0 + (y1 - y0) * j / ny\n        for i in range(nx + 1):\n            x = x0 + (x1 - x0) * i / nx\n            if i in (0, nx) or j in (0, ny):\n                z = -0.45\n            else:\n                z = _mound_h(x, y) + rng.uniform(-0.22, 0.14)\n                z = max(z, -0.20)\n            verts[(i, j)] = bm.verts.new((x, y, z))\n    bm.verts.ensure_lookup_table()\n    for j in range(ny):\n        for i in range(nx):\n            bm.faces.new((verts[(i, j)], verts[(i + 1, j)],\n                          verts[(i + 1, j + 1)], verts[(i, j + 1)]))\n    rim = [verts[(i, 0)] for i in range(nx + 1)] + \\\n          [verts[(nx, j)] for j in range(1, ny + 1)] + \\\n          [verts[(i, ny)] for i in range(nx - 1, -1, -1)] + \\\n          [verts[(0, j)] for j in range(ny - 1, 0, -1)]\n    bm.faces.new(rim)\n\n    # 坡面几何建完之后再建 BVH，否则 ray_cast 全打空\n    bvh = BVHTree.FromBMesh(bm)\n\n    def surf_at(x, y):\n        hit = bvh.ray_cast(Vector((x, y, 40.0)), Vector((0.0, 0.0, -1.0)))\n        return None if hit[0] is None else hit[0].z\n\n    def surf_max(x, y, r):\n        best = None\n        for k in range(6):\n            a = 2.0 * math.pi * k / 6.0\n            z = surf_at(x + r * math.cos(a), y + r * math.sin(a))\n            if z is not None and (best is None or z > best):\n                best = z\n        return best\n\n    def foot_pts(x, y, size, rot):\n        m = Euler(rot, \"XYZ\").to_matrix()\n        pts = [(x, y)]\n        for sx in (-0.5, 0.5):\n            for sy in (-0.5, 0.5):\n                v = m @ Vector((sx * size[0], sy * size[1], 0.0))\n                pts.append((x + v.x, y + v.y))\n        return pts\n\n    def seat(x, y, size, rot, sink):\n        zs = [surf_at(px, py) for (px, py) in foot_pts(x, y, size, rot)]\n        zs = [z for z in zs if z is not None]\n        if not zs:\n            return None\n        return min(zs) - sink + _rot_low(size, rot)\n\n    # 三档碎块：长边 0.15 / 0.40 / 0.90 m\n    for (long_edge, ratio) in RUBBLE_CLASSES:\n        count = max(1, int(round(260 * ratio)))\n        for _ in range(count):\n            sx = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.55, 1.0)\n            sy = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.45, 1.0)\n            sz = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.22, 0.55)\n            rot = (rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5), rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5), rng.uniform(0.0, 3.14))\n            x = rng.uniform(-2.45, 3.85)\n            y = rng.uniform(-5.10, 5.70)\n            surf = surf_max(x, y, 0.5 * max(sx, sy))\n            if surf is None or surf < (0.22 if long_edge < 0.5 else 0.30):\n                continue\n            size = (sx, sy, sz)\n            zc = seat(x, y, size, rot, sink=0.10 if long_edge > 0.5 else 0.14)\n            if zc is None:\n                continue\n            mat_idx = 1 if rng.random() < 0.28 else 0\n            _box(bm, Vector((x, y, zc)), size, rot=rot, rng=rng, jitter=0.03,\n                 mat_idx=mat_idx)\n\n    # 5 块大残板（半埋、带倾角，互相有支撑关系）\n    for _ in range(5):\n        sx = rng.uniform(1.10, 2.10)\n        sy = rng.uniform(0.70, 1.30)\n        sz = rng.uniform(0.16, 0.24)\n        rot = (rng.uniform(-0.65, 0.65), rng.uniform(-0.45, 0.45), rng.uniform(0.0, 3.14))\n        x = rng.uniform(-1.30, 2.90)\n        y = rng.uniform(-3.40, 3.80)\n        surf = surf_max(x, y, 0.5 * max(sx, sy))\n        if surf is None or surf < 0.75:\n            continue\n        size = (sx, sy, sz)\n        zc = seat(x, y, size, rot, sink=0.16)\n        if zc is None:\n            continue\n        _box(bm, Vector((x, y, zc)), size, rot=rot, rng=rng, jitter=0.05)\n\n    # 坡脚散砖（压在坡体足迹内，落在地面以下，不会悬空）\n    for _ in range(46):\n        sx = rng.uniform(0.16, 0.34)\n        sy = rng.uniform(0.12, 0.26)\n        sz = rng.uniform(0.07, 0.14)\n        rot = (rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35), rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35), rng.uniform(0.0, 3.14))\n        x = rng.uniform(-2.60, 3.90)\n        y = rng.uniform(-5.30, 5.90)\n        surf = surf_max(x, y, 0.25)\n        if surf is None or surf < 0.10:\n            continue\n        size = (sx, sy, sz)\n        zc = -0.20 + _rot_low(size, ---------------------------------------------------------------- 楼板残段\n\ndef _slab_01():\n    \"\"\"二层楼板残段：从后墙塌下来，一头仍咬在后墙里，另一头落在楼底。\"\"\"\n    bm = bmesh.new()\n    rng = random.Random(SEED + 4)\n    ang = math.radians(32.0)\n    mat = Matrix.Translation((-3.95, 3.30, 0.42)) @ Matrix.Rotation(ang, 4, \"X\")\n    w, ln = 3.90, 6.60\n    cell = 0.40\n    xs, dx = _axis(w, cell)\n    ys, dy = rng.random() < 0.45:\n                ok = False\n            if ok and v > 0.72 and rng.random() < 0.30:\n                ok = False\n            mask[j][i] = ok\n    _break_pinches(mask, nx, ny)\n    _keep_largest_island(mask, nx, ny)\n    _skin(bm, xs, ys, mask, 0.0, SLAB_T, rng, 0.05)\n    _merge_into(bm, mat)\n    return bm\n\n\ndef _slab_02():\n    \"\"\"斜插在坡脚的破损板。\"\"\"\n    bm =         if ok and rng.random() < 0.18:\n                ok = False\n            mask[j][i] = ok\n    _break_pinches(mask, nx, nz)\n    _keep_largest_island(mask, nx, nz)\n    _skin(bm, xs, zs, mask,  # 直径 1.5 cm\n\nREBARS = [\n    # (起点(局部), 主方向, 弯曲方向, 长度)\n    ((-5.35, -3.86, 6.35), (-0.25, -0.55, 0.80), (0.55, -0.35, 0.10), 0.62),\n    ((-4.10, -3.86, 6.10), (0.20, -0.72, 0.66), (0.45, -0.25, + 1):\n        t = i / n\n        p = start + d * (length * t) + b * (length * (t ** 1.9))\n        p = p + Vector((rng.uniform(-0.015, 0.015), rng.uniform(-0.015, 0.015),\n                        rng.uniform(-0.015, 0.015)))\n        pts.append(p)\n    tip = pts[-1]\n    last = (pts[-1] - pts[-2]).normalized()\n    side = last.cross(Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0)))\n    if side.length < 1e-4:\n        side = Vector((1.0, 0.0, 0.0))\n    side.normalize()\n    pts.append(tip + side * hook)\n    pts.append(tip + side * hook - last * (hook * 0.6))\n    return pts\n\n\ndef _rebar_obj(idx):\n    bm = bmesh.new()\n    rng = random.Random(SEED + 40 + idx)\n    start, d, b, length = REBARS[idx]\n    root = Vector(start) - Vector(d).normalized() * REBAR_BURY\n    pts = [root] + - quantize the heightfield z to 0.12 m steps and add per-cell jitter ±0.25 → blocky, faceted.
- Actually a nicer trick: build the mound as a *stack of boxes* rather than a heightfield! I.e., 200-300 large blocks placed with a "prop upward" rule: for each block, place it on the surface of the pile accumulated so far... that's a growth simulation: start with the mound heightfield as a base, then drop blocks onto the *current* surface, updating the heightfield. That would look like a real pile with block steps.

Implement: keep a coarse grid H[y][x] (e.g., 0.35 m cells) initialized from _mound_h. Then for each block: pick (x,y), take H at that cell, place the block's bottom at H - sink, and update H in the cells it covers to the block's top (minus a bit). This is a simple "particle deposition" → produces a block-pile with terraces ✓ very much like the reference.

That's a good upgrade: the mound becomes blocks-on-blocks, and the heightfield surface can even be *replaced* by the block stack (no smooth dome). But without the heightfield the pile might have holes. Keep the heightfield as the base (it's the fine debris) but lower it (2.4 m) and let blocks build up to 3 m on top.

Given "体量再加 40%", the pile volume increases through the block stacking ✓.

Let me estimate tris: 400 blocks × 12 = 4800; heightfield 1000; total ~6000 → total scene ~14000. Fits.

Plan for `_rubble` rewrite:
- Grid H (nx=26, ny=40) over x -3.6..4.8, y -5.6..6.2, cell 0.31.
- Initialize H[i][j] = _mound_h(x,y) with noise, clipped ≥ -0.4 (below ground → buried).
- Build the heightfield mesh from H (as now, with skirt + bottom).
- Build BVH from the heightfield for initial ray casts? For block deposition I'll use the H grid directly (bilinear sample) updated as blocks are added — simpler and robust. But then a block placed on the *interpolated* H may float above the actual triangulated mesh by a few mm — previously this caused floaters. Since I now update H with the block's own top, subsequent blocks reference H; the first layer would rest on the triangulated surface → the same 3-56 mm issue.

Fix: place blocks with a generous sink (0.10-0.18) relative to H, and H is a *lower bound* of the mesh surface? Not guaranteed.

Alternative: keep the BVH approach for the base (accurate) and for stacked blocks use the BVH of the *already placed blocks + heightfield*, rebuilt periodically. BVHTree.FromBMesh costs O(n) build; rebuilding after every block (400 times) is too slow. Rebuild every 20 blocks (20 rebuilds) — each rebuild on ~5000 faces is fast (~ms). That works and gives accurate seating!

Simpler: since blocks are placed sequentially, and each new block's support comes from previously placed geometry, I can rebuild the BVH every 25 blocks and raycast from above. Between rebuilds, the newest blocks aren't in the BVH → new blocks might seat on the old surface (below) and interpenetrate the newer blocks — that's fine (interpenetration = support ✓, looks like stacked rubble).

Edit A (mound):

```
def _mound_h(x, y):
    """碎砖坡高度场：中段最高 ~3.05 m，向四周衰减到 0。"""
    xc, yc = 0.70, 0.30
    ax, ay = 3.45, 6.60
    t = 1.0 - ((x - xc) / ax) ** 2 - ((y - yc) / ay) ** 2
    if t <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return 3.05 * (t ** 0.85)


RUBBLE_CLASSES = ((0.15, 0.11), (0.40, 0.40), (0.90, 0.19))
```
new: base mound 2.55 (blocks stack on top), and classes with counts.

For Edit C old_string: from "    # 三档碎块：长边 0.15 / 0.40 / 0.90 m" through the end of the "5 块大残板" loop? Let me replace only the 三档碎块 loop (up to before "# 5 块大残板").

New loop:
```python
    # 三档碎块：长边 0.15 / 0.40 / 0.90 m，逐块"沉积"——每块坐在当时的坡面上，
    # 每 25 块重建一次 BVH，于是上面的块会坐在下面的块上（互相支撑、台阶感）。
    state = {"bvh": bvh, "n": 0}

    def fresh_bvh():
        state["bvh"] = BVHTree.FromBMesh(bm)

    def surf(x, y, r):
        b = state["bvh"]
        best = None
        for k in range(7):
            if k == 0:
                px, py = x, y
            else:
                a = 2.0 * math.pi * (k - 1) / 6.0
                px, py = x + r * math.cos(a), y + r * math.sin(a)
            hit = b.ray_cast(Vector((px, py, 40.0)), Vector((0.0, 0.0, -1.0)))
            if hit[0] is not None and (best is None or hit[0].z > best):
                best = hit[0].z
        return best

    for (long_edge, count) in RUBBLE_CLASSES:
        for _ in range(count):
            sx = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.55, 1.0)
            sy = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.45, 1.0)
            sz = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.25, 0.60)
            rot = (rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9), rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9), rng.uniform(0.0, 3.14))
            x = rng.uniform(-2.80, 4.10)
            y = rng.uniform(-5.10, 5.75)
            if state["n"] % 25 == 0:
                fresh_bvh()
            top = surf(x, y, 0.5 * max(sx, sy))
            if top is None or top < 0.12:
                continue
            size = (sx, sy, sz)
            zc = top - (0.16 if long_edge > 0.5 else 0.11) + _rot_low(size, rot)
            mat_idx = 1 if rng.random() < 0.28 else 0
            _box(bm, Vector((x, y, zc)), size, rot=rot, rng=rng, jitter=0.035,
                 mat_idx=mat_idx)
            state["n"] += 1
```
Note the `surf` closure shadows nothing (we don't have another surf). It uses `state["bvh"]`, but `bvh` defined earlier as the heightfield BVH ✓.

Careful: `_rot_low(size, rot)` — with rot up to ±0.9 the vertical half-height can exceed sz, so zc = top - sink + low puts the block's *lowest* point at top-sink → half buried relative to the local max hit. Since `top` is the max over the footprint, the block's bottom is around top-sink while its lowest corner is at top-sink... yes: center = top - sink + low → lowest point z = top - sink ✓ so it sinks by `sink` below the highest footprint point → contact ✓ good.

    # 三档碎块：长边 0.15 / 0.40 / 0.90 m

            x = rng.uniform(-2.45, 3.85)

            surf = surf_max(x, y, 0.5 * max(sx, sy))
            if surf is None or surf < (0.22 if long_edge < 0.5 else 0.30):

            size = (sx, sy, sz)

                continue
            mat_idx = 1 if rng.random() < 0.28 else 0

New text:

```
    # 三档碎块：长边 0.15 / 0.40 / 0.90 m。逐块"沉积"：每块坐在当时的坡面上，
    # 每 25 块重建一次 BVH，于是后放的块会坐在先放的块上（互相支撑、台阶感）。
    state = {"bvh": bvh, "n": 0}

    def pile_top(x, y, r):
        b = state["bvh"]
        best = None
        for k in range(7):
            if k == 0:
                px, py = x, y
            else:
                a = 2.0 * math.pi * (k - 1) / 6.0
                px, py = x + r * math.cos(a), y + r * math.sin(a)
            hit = b.ray_cast(Vector((px, py, 40.0)), Vector((0.0, 0.0, -1.0)))
            if hit[0] is not None and (best is None or hit[0].z > best):
                best = hit[0].z
        return best

    for (long_edge, count) in RUBBLE_CLASSES:
        for _ in range(count):
            sx = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.55, 1.0)
            sy = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.45, 1.0)
            sz = long_edge * rng.uniform(0.25, 0.60)
            rot = (rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85), rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85), rng.uniform(0.0, 3.14))
            x = rng.uniform(-2.80, 4.15)
            y = rng.uniform(-5.10, 5.75)
            if state["n"] % 25 == 0:
                state["bvh"] = BVHTree.FromBMesh(bm)
            top = pile_top(x, y, 0.5 * max(sx, sy))
            if top is None or top < 0.12:
                continue
            size = (sx, sy, sz)
            zc = top - (0.17 if long_edge > 0.5 else 0.11) + _rot_low(size, rot)
            _box(bm, Vector((x, y, zc)), size, rot=rot, rng=rng, jitter=0.035,
                 mat_idx=1 if rng.random() < 0.28 else 0)
            state["n"] += 1
```

        b = state["bvh"]
        lo = None; hi = None

                lo = z if lo is None or z < lo else lo

        if hi is None: return None
        return 0.5 * (lo + hi)

---------------------------------------------------------------- 网格骨架\n\ndef _merge(dst, src, mat):\n    \"\"\"把 src bmesh 中的几何按 mat 变换后并入 dst。\"\"\"\n    vmap = {}\n    for v in src.verts:\n        vmap[v] = dst.verts.new(mat @ v.co)\n    for f in src.faces:\n        try:\n            dst.faces.new([vmap[v] for v in f.verts])\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    src.free()\n\n\ndef _axis(w, cell):\n    n = max(2, int(round(w / cell)))\n    dx = w / n\n    return [i * dx for i in range(n + 1)], dx\n\n\ndef _zaxis(h, cell, band=None):\n    \"\"\"z 轴：均匀网格；(band) 边界插一对相隔 4 cm 的行 → 楼板带台阶是硬棱不是斜面。\"\"\"\n    n = max(2, int(round(h / cell)))\n    dz = h / n\n    zs = [i * dz for i in range(n + 1)]\n    if band:\n        extra = []\n        for zb in band:\n            if 0.0 < zb < h:\n                extra += [zb - 0.02, zb + 0.02]\n        if extra:\n            zs = sorted(set([round(v, 5) for v in zs] + [round(v, 5) for v in extra]))\n    return zs\n\n\ndef _top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h):\n    \"\"\"墙顶轮廓（每列保留几格）：2–4 段宽平台 + 2–4 处大崩口 + 少量 1 格小齿。\n\n    只允许\"1 列宽的细高片\"存在到 ±2 格：细高片在成片里就是一张发亮薄卡片。\n    \"\"\"\n    dz = h / nz\n    H = [nz] * nx\n    nseg = max(2, min(4, nx // 4))\n    if nx >= 8 and nseg > 1:\n        cuts = sorted(rng.sample(range(3, nx - 2), nseg - 1))\n        idx, cur = 0, nz\n        for i in range(nx):\n            while idx < len(cuts) and i == cuts[idx]:\n                cur = min(nz, max(3, cur + rng.randint(-5, 5)))\n                idx += 1\n            H[i] = cur\n\n    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 4)):\n        wd = rng.randint(2, max(2, nx // 4))\n        dep = rng.randint(2, max(2, int(round(jag / dz)) + 2))\n        x0 = rng.randint(0, max(0, nx - wd))\n        for i in range(x0, min(nx, x0 + wd)):\n            H[i] = max(3, H[i] - dep)\n\n    # 一处深崩口（打到楼板带，露出楼板断面）\n    wd = rng.randint(2, max(2, nx // 5))\n    x0 = rng.randint(nx // 2, max(nx // 2, nx - wd))\n    for i in range(x0, min(nx, x0 + wd)):\n        H[i] = max(3, H[i] - int(round(nz * 0.55)))\n\n    for i in range(nx):\n        if rng.random() < 0.25:\n            H[i] = min(nz, max(3, H[i] + rng.choice((-1, 1))))\n\n    span = max(1.0, nx * 0.22)\n    for i in range(nx):\n        fl = max(0.0, 1.0 - i / span)\n        fr = max(0.0, 1.0 - (nx - 1 - i) / span)\n        H[i] -= int(round((drop_left * fl + drop_right * fr) / dz))\n        H[i] = max(3, min(nz, H[i]))\n\n    for _ in range(8):\n        changed = False\n        for i in range(nx):\n            lo = H[i - 1] if i > 0 else H[i]\n            hi = H[i + 1] if i < nx - 1 else H[i]\n            lim = min(lo, hi) + 2\n            if H[i] > lim:\n                H[i] = lim\n                changed = True\n        if not changed:\n            break\n    return [H[i] * dz for i in range(nx)]\n\n\ndef _mask(xs, zs, tops, holes, rng, erode_side=0.0):\n    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1\n    mask = [[False] * nx for _ in range(nz)]\n    for j in range(nz):\n        cz = 0.5 * (zs[j] + zs[j + 1])\n        for i in range(nx):\n            cx = 0.5 * (xs[i] + xs[i + 1])\n            ok = cz <= tops[i]\n            if ok and erode_side > 0.0 and (i == 0 or i == nx - 1):\n                if rng.random() < erode_side:\n                    ok = False\n            if ok:\n                for (ax, bx, az, bz) in holes:\n                    if ax < cx < bx and az < cz < bz:\n                        ok = False\n                        break\n            mask[j][i] = ok\n    return mask\n\n\ndef _break_pinches(mask, nx, nz):\n    \"\"\"拆掉\"只在一个角点上相接\"的格子对（否则加厚后出现 4 面共边的非流形棱）。\"\"\"\n    for _ in range(8):\n        changed = False\n        for j in range(1, nz):\n            for i in range(1, nx):\n                a, b = mask[j - 1][i - 1], mask[j - 1][i]\n                c, d = mask[j][i - 1], mask[j][i]\n                if a and d and not b and not c:\n                    mask[j][i] = False\n                    changed = True\n                elif b and c and not a and not d:\n                    mask[j - 1][i] = False\n                    changed = True\n        if not changed:\n            return\n\n\ndef _keep_largest_island(mask, nx, nz):\n    \"\"\"只留最大的一块连通区域（拆夹点可能孤立出 1–2 格，加厚后就是浮碎片）。\"\"\"\n    seen = [[False] * nx for _ in range(nz)]\n    keep = set()\n    for j0 in range(nz):\n        for i0 in range(nx):\n            if not mask[j0][i0] or seen[j0][i0]:\n                continue\n            seen[j0][i0] = True\n            stack = [(j0, i0)]\n            comp = []\n            while stack:\n                j, i = stack.pop()\n                comp.append((j, i))\n                for dj, di in ((1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, 1), (0, -1)):\n                    jj, ii = j + dj, i + di\n                    if 0 <= jj < nz and 0 <= ii < nx and mask[jj][ii] and not seen[jj][ii]:\n                        seen[jj][ii] = True\n                        stack.append((jj, ii))\n            if len(comp) > len(keep):\n                keep = set(comp)\n    for j in range(nz):\n        for i in range(nx):\n            if mask[j][i] and (j, i) not in keep:\n                mask[j][i] = False\n\n\ndef _band_shift(zs, band, sign):\n    \"\"\"楼板带外凸量（按顶点 z）：只在带内、离边界 2 cm 以上的行外凸。\"\"\"\n    if not band:\n        return None\n    b0, b1 = band\n    return [sign * BAND_OUT if (b0 + 0.019 < z < b1 - 0.019) else 0.0 for z in zs]\n\n\ndef _skin(bm, xs, zs, mask, y0, thick, rng, jitter, shift=None, back_shift=None):\n    \"\"\"把 mask 的格子做成一块厚 thick 的闭合板：正面 y=y0(+shift)，背面 y=y0+thick(+back_shift)。\"\"\"\n    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1\n    fv, bv = {}, {}\n\n    def fvert(i, j):\n        k = (i, j)\n        v = fv.get(k)\n        if v is None:\n            dy = shift[j] if shift else 0.0\n            v = bm.verts.new((xs[i] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter),\n                              y0 + dy + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) * 0.9,\n                              zs[j] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter)))\n            fv[k] = v\n        return v\n\n    def bvert(i, j):\n        k = (i, j)\n        v = bv.get(k)\n        if v is None:\n            dy = back_shift[j] if back_shift else 0.0\n            v = bm.verts.new((xs[i] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter),\n                              y0 + thick + dy + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) * 0.9,\n                              zs[j] + rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter)))\n            bv[k] = v\n        return v\n\n    def solid(i, j):\n        return 0 <= i < nx and 0 <= j < nz and mask[j][i]\n\n    for j in range(nz):\n        for i in range(nx):\n            if not mask[j][i]:\n                continue\n            try:\n                bm.faces.new((fvert(i, j), fvert(i, j + 1), fvert(i + 1, j + 1), fvert(i + 1, j)))\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n            try:\n                bm.faces.new((bvert(i + 1, j), bvert(i + 1, j + 1), bvert(i, j + 1), bvert(i, j)))\n            except ValueError:\n                pass\n            # 侧壁：只在与空格相邻的一侧补，保证闭合且法线可 recalc\n            if not solid(i - 1, j):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i, j), bvert(i, j), bvert(i, j + 1), fvert(i, j + 1)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            if not solid(i + 1, j):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i + 1, j + 1), bvert(i + 1, j + 1), bvert(i + 1, j), fvert(i + 1, j)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            if not solid(i, j - 1):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i + 1, j), bvert(i + 1, j), bvert(i, j), fvert(i, j)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n            if not solid(i, j + 1):\n                try:\n                    bm.faces.new((fvert(i, j + 1), bvert(i, j + 1), bvert(i + 1, j + 1), fvert(i + 1, j + 1)))\n                except ValueError:\n                    pass\n\n    loose = [v for v in bm.verts if not v.link_faces]\n    if loose:\n        bmesh.ops.delete(bm, geom=loose, context=\"VERTS\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 小构件\n\ndef _box(bm, center, size, rot=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0), rng=None, jitter=0.0, mat_idx=0):\n    \"\"\"一个随机旋转/顶点抖动的盒子（12 三角）。\"\"\"\n    sx, sy, sz = size\n    mat = Matrix.Translation(center) @ Euler(rot, \"XYZ\").to_matrix().to_4x4()\n    corners = []\n    for dz in (-0.5, 0.5):\n        for dy in (-0.5, 0.5):\n            for dx in (-0.5, 0.5):\n                jx = rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) if rng else 0.0\n                jy = rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) if rng else 0.0\n                jz = rng.uniform(-jitter, jitter) if rng else 0.0\n                corners.append(bm.verts.new(mat @ Vector((dx * sx + jx, dy * sy + jy, dz * sz + jz))))\n    quads = ((0, 1, 3, 2), (4, 6, 7, 5), (0, 4, 5, 1), (2, 3, 7, 6), (0, 2, 6, 4), (1, 5, 7, 3))\n    for q in quads:\n        f = bm.faces.new([corners[i] for i in q])\n        f.material_index = mat_idx\n\n\ndef _tri_prism(bm, p0, p1, p2, nrm, t=0.012):\n    \"\"\"薄三角片（碎玻璃）：两点面 + 三条侧边，闭合 8 三角。\"\"\"\n    a = [bm.verts.new(p + nrm * (t * 0.5)) for p in (p0, p1, p2)]\n    b = [bm.verts.new(p - nrm * (t * 0.5)) for p in (p0, p1, p2)]\n    bm.faces.new(a)\n    bm.faces.new(list(reversed(b)))\n    for i in range(3):\n        j = (i + 1) % 3\n        bm.faces.new((a[i], b[i], b[j], a[j]))\n\n\ndef _tube(bm, pts, radius, sides=5):\n    \"\"\"沿折线扫掠的圆管（两端封盖）。\"\"\"\n    up = Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0))\n    rings = []\n    for i, p in enumerate(pts):\n        if i == 0:\n            d = pts[1] - pts[0]\n        elif i == len(pts) - 1:\n            d = pts[-1] - pts[-2]\n        else:\n            d = pts[i + 1] - pts[i - 1]\n        if d.length < 1e-6:\n            d = Vector((0.0, 0.0, 1.0))\n        d = d.normalized()\n        ref = up if abs(d.dot(up)) < 0.9 else Vector((1.0, 0.0, 0.0))\n        u = d.cross(ref).normalized()\n        v = d.cross(u).normalized()\n        ring = []\n        for k in range(sides):\n            a = 2.0 * math.pi * k / sides\n            ring.append(bm.verts.new(p + u * (radius * math.cos(a)) + v * (radius * math.sin(a))))\n        rings.append(ring)\n    for i in range(len(rings) - 1):\n        a, b = rings[i], rings[i + 1]\n        for k in range(sides):\n            k2 = (k + 1) % sides\n            bm.faces.new((a[k], a[k2], b[k2], b[k]))\n    bm.faces.new(list(reversed(rings[0])))\n    bm.faces.new(rings[-1])\n\n\ndef _rot_low(size, rot):\n    m = Euler(rot, \"XYZ\").to_matrix()\n    sx, sy, sz = size\n    return 0.5 * (abs(m[2][0]) * sx + abs(m[2][1]) * sy + abs(m[2][2]) * sz)\n\n\ndef _lean(bm, kx, ky):\n    \"\"\"按高度剪切：造成 1–4° 倾角。\"\"\"\n    for v in bm.verts:\n        z = v.co.z\n        if z > 0.0:\n            v.co.x += kx * z\n            v.co.y += ky * z\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 出对象\n\ndef _ensure_collection():\n    root = bpy.data.collections.get(ROOT_COLLECTION)\n    if root is None:\n        root = bpy.data.collections.new(ROOT_COLLECTION)\n        bpy.context.scene.collection.children.link(root)\n    coll = bpy.data.collections.get(COLLECTION)\n    if coll is None:\n        coll = bpy.data.collections.new(COLLECTION)\n        root.children.link(coll)\n    return coll\n\n\ndef _purge():\n    for ob in list(bpy.data.objects):\n        if ob.name.startswith(PREFIX):\n            me = ob.data\n            bpy.data.objects.remove(ob, do_unlink=True)\n            if me is not None and me.users == 0:\n                bpy.data.meshes.remove(me)\n    for me in list(bpy.data.meshes):\n        if me.users == 0:\n            bpy.data.meshes.remove(me)\n    for mat in list(bpy.data.materials):\n        if mat.name.startswith(\"wst_mat_\") and mat.users == 0:\n            bpy.data.materials.remove(mat)\n\n\ndef _shell_signed_volume(faces):\n    vol = 0.0\n    for f in faces:\n        vs = f.verts\n        a = vs[0].co\n        for k in range(1, len(vs) - 1):\n            b, c = vs[k].co, vs[k + 1].co\n            vol += a.dot(b.cross(c)) / 6.0\n    return vol\n\n\ndef _fix_shell_normals(bm):\n    \"\"\"按连通分量校正法线：体积为负说明整片壳朝内（recalc 对薄壳会判反）。\"\"\"\n    bm.faces.ensure_lookup_table()\n    seen = set()\n    for f0 in bm.faces:\n        if f0 in seen:\n            continue\n        comp, stack = [], [f0]\n        seen.add(f0)\n        while stack:\n            f = stack.pop()\n            comp.append(f)\n            for e in f.edges:\n                for g in e.link_faces:\n                    if g not in seen:\n                        seen.add(g)\n                        stack.append(g)\n        if _shell_signed_volume(comp) < 0.0:\n            bmesh.ops.reverse_faces(bm, faces=comp)\n\n\ndef _to_object(name, bm, mats, coll):\n    bm.normal_update()\n    bmesh.ops.recalc_face_normals(bm, faces=bm.faces[:])\n    _fix_shell_normals(bm)\n    me = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)\n    bm.to_mesh(me)\n    bm.free()\n    for m in mats:\n        me.materials.append(m)\n    ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name, me)\n    ob.location = LOC\n    ob.rotation_euler = (0.0, 0.0, ROT_Z)\n    coll.objects.link(ob)\n    return ob\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 墙（两层皮 + 楼板带）\n\ndef _hole_bounds(mask, xs, zs, hole):\n    \"\"\"取某窗口实际吃掉的格子范围 → 洞壁的真实平面坐标（附带格子索引）。\"\"\"\n    ax, bx, az, bz = hole\n    i0 = i1 = None\n    j0 = j1 = None\n    nx, nz = len(xs) - 1, len(zs) - 1\n    for j in range(nz):\n        cz = 0.5 * (zs[j] + zs[j + 1])\n        for i in range(nx):\n            cx = 0.5 * (xs[i] + xs[i + 1])\n            if ax < cx < bx and az < cz < bz and not mask[j][i]:\n                i0 = i if i0 is None else min(i0, i)\n                i1 = i if i1 is None else max(i1, i)\n                j0 = j if j0 is None else min(j0, j)\n                j1 = j if j1 is None else max(j1, j)\n    if i0 is None:\n        return None\n    return (xs[i0], xs[i1 + 1], zs[j0], zs[j1 + 1], i0, i1, j0, j1)\n\n\ndef _window_dress(bm, rng, bounds, mask):\n    \"\"\"残存窗框细条 + 歪斜碎玻璃（都长在 0.14 深的窗洞里，贴着洞壁）。\n\n    只有洞的四边确实还有墙体时才装：否则窗框会孤零零挂在空中（\"浮块\"）。\n    \"\"\"\n    x0, x1, z0, z1, i0, i1, j0, j1 = bounds\n    w = x1 - x0\n    h = z1 - z0\n    if w < 0.55 or h < 0.55:\n        return\n    nz = len(mask)\n    nx = len(mask[0])\n    bottom_ok = j0 - 1 >= 0 and all(mask[j0 - 1][i] for i in range(i0, i1 + 1))\n    top_ok = j1 + 1 < nz and all(mask[j1 + 1][i] for i in range(i0, i1 + 1))\n    left_ok = i0 - 1 >= 0 and all(mask[j][i0 - 1] for j in range(j0, j1 + 1))\n    right_ok = i1 + 1 < nx and all(mask[j][i1 + 1] for j in range(j0, j1 + 1))\n    ymid = FRONT_SKIN * 0.45\n    if not bottom_ok:\n        return\n    # 下槛（横向变形条）\n    _box(bm, Vector((0.5 * (x0 + x1), ymid, z0 + 0.045)),\n         (w, FRONT_SKIN * 0.7, 0.09), rot=(0.0, rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03), 0.0))\n    # 一根竖梃（一端压在下槛上）/ 一根上框残条\n    if left_ok or right_ok:\n        jx = x0 + w * rng.uniform(0.25, 0.7)\n        _box(bm, Vector((jx, ymid, z0 + h * 0.42)),\n             (0.055, FRONT_SKIN * 0.55, h * 0.62), rot=(rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05), 0.0, 0.0))\n    if top_ok:\n        _box(bm, Vector((x0 + w * 0.5, ymid, z1 - 0.05)),\n             (w * 0.55, FRONT_SKIN * 0.5, 0.055), rot=(0.0, rng.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), 0.0))\n    # 碎玻璃：底边贴在下槛顶面（z0+0.09），三片歪斜三角形\n    for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 3)):\n        bx = x0 + w * rng.uniform(0.12, 0.78)\n        by = 0.02 + rng.uniform(0.0, 0.06)\n        bw = w * rng.uniform(0.12, 0.3)\n        bh = h * rng.uniform(0.12, 0.34)\n        p0 = Vector((bx, by, z0 + 0.09))\n        p1 = Vector((bx + bw, by + rng.uniform(-0.02, 0.02), z0 + 0.09))\n        p2 = Vector((bx + bw * rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8), by + rng.uniform(-0.01, 0.03),\n                     z0 + 0.09 + bh))\n        _tri_prism(bm, p0, p1, p2, Vector((0.0, 1.0, 0.0)), t=0.010)\n\n\ndef _wall(bm, mat, w, top, holes, rng, cell=CELL_FRONT, through=(),\n          jag=1.1, drop_left=0.0, drop_right=0.0, erode_side=0.0,\n          jitter=0.045, band=(BAND_Z0, BAND_Z1), dress=(), rear=True):\n    \"\"\"一面墙：前皮（开窗口袋）+ 后皮（只有穿透洞）+ 楼板带外凸。\n\n    z 从 -BURY 到 top；y 从 0（外表面）到 WALL_T。hole 坐标是建筑局部 (x, z)。\n    \"\"\"\n    h = top + BURY\n    xs, dx = _axis(w, cell)\n    nx = len(xs) - 1\n    zs = _zaxis(h, cell, (band[0] + BURY, band[1] + BURY) if band else None)\n    nz = len(zs) - 1\n    tops = _top_profile(nx, nz, rng, jag, drop_left, drop_right, h)\n    holes_p = [(a, b, c + BURY, d + BURY) for (a, b, c, d) in holes]\n    through_p = [holes_p[i] for i in through if 0 <= i < len(holes_p)]\n\n    m_f = _mask(xs, zs, tops, holes_p, rng, erode_side)\n    _break_pinches(m_f, nx, nz)\n    _keep_largest_island(m_f, nx, nz)\n    _skin(bm, xs, zs, m_f, 0.0, FRONT_SKIN, rng, jitter,\n          shift=_band_shift(zs, tuple(v + BURY for v in band) if band else None, -1.0))\n\n    if rear:\n        xs2, dx2 = _axis(w, CELL_REAR)\n        nx2 = len(xs2) - 1\n        zs2 = _zaxis(h, CELL_REAR, tuple(v + BURY for v in band) if band else None)\n        nz2 = len(zs2) - 1\n        tops2 = []\n        for i in range(nx2):\n            cx = 0.5 * (xs2[i] + xs2[i + 1])\n            k = min(nx - 1, max(0, int(cx / dx)))\n            tops2.append(tops[k] - 0.10)\n        m_r = _mask(xs2, zs2, tops2, through_p, rng, 0.0)\n        _break_pinches(m_r, nx2, nz2)\n        _keep_largest_island(m_r, nx2, nz2)\n        _skin(bm, xs2, zs2, m_r, REAR_Y0, REAR_T, rng, jitter * 1.5,\n              back_shift=_band_shift(zs2, tuple(v + BURY for v in band) if band else None, 1.0))\n\n    # 窗户配件（窗框残条 / 碎玻璃）\n    for hi in dress:\n        if 0 <= hi < len(holes_p):\n            b = _hole_bounds(m_f, xs, zs, holes_p[hi])\n            if b:\n                _window_dress(bm, rng, b, m_f)\n\n    _merge_into(bm, mat)\n\n\ndef _merge_into(bm, mat):\n    \"\"\"把 bm 里当前全部几何按 mat 变换（就地，用于把面板搬到墙位）。\"\"\"\n    if mat is None:\n        return\n    for v in bm.verts:\n        v.co = mat @ v.co\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 各部件\n\ndef _left_block():\n    \"\"\"左段：正面开窗两层残墙 + 可见左端墙 + 后墙残段。\"\"\"\n    bm = bmesh.new()\n    rng = random.Random(SEED + 1)\n    w = LEFT_X1 - LEFT_X0\n\n    holes = [\n        (0.38, 1.23, 1.00, 2.70),\n        (1.78, 2.63, 0.95, 2.65),\n        (3.18, 4.03, 1.10, 2.75),\n        (0.50, 1.45, 4.05, 5.65),\n        (1.95, 2.85, 4.00, 5.60),\n        (3.25, 4.65, 4.15, 5.70),\n    ]\n    sub = bmesh.new()\n    _wall(sub, None, w, LEFT_TOP, holes, rng, cell=CELL_FRONT,\n          through=(1, 4), BACK_Y - y\n    sub = bmesh.new()\n    _wall(sub, None, 8.0, LEFT_TOP - 0.15,\n          [(4.30, 5.50, 1.20, 2.60),\n           (1.60, 2.80, 3.95, 5.35),\n           (6.55, 7.45, 4.10, 5.55)],\n          rng, cell=CELL_SIDE, through=(2,), jag=0.95,\n          drop_left=0.55, drop_right=0.30, jitter=0.05, dress=(1,))\n    _merge(bm, sub, Matrix.Translation((LEFT_X0, BACK_Y, 0.0)) @\n           Matrix.Rotation(-math.pi / 2.0, 4, \"Z\") @ Matrix.Translation((0.0, 0.0, -BURY)))\n\n    # 后墙残段（大半塌掉，只作背光剪影）\n    sub = bmesh.new()\n    _wall(sub, None, w, 5.40,\n          [(0.55, 1.95, 0.60, 2.60), (2.60, 3.95, 1.30, 3.10)],\n          rng, cell=CELL_SIDE, jag=2.30, drop_left=1.10, drop_right=0.60,\n          erode_side=0.45, jitter=0.06)\n    _merge(bm, sub, Matrix.Translation((LEFT_X0,
